Let's analyze coordinate descent in detail for polynomial convolution.
Given a≥0 sum S. c = conv(a,a) length m=2n-1. Objective F=2n max(c)/S^2; with S fixed = sqrt(2n), F=max(c). Suppose we transfer δ from i to j (i source, j target; i≠j), a_i' = a_i - δ, a_j'=a_j+δ, 0≤δ≤a_i. New c' = c + 2δ (v_j - v_i), where v_j[k] = a_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n else 0. Let d = 2(v_j - v_i). For a set of candidate target lags TopU (current near max) and maybe all lags, max(c+δ d) over δ∈[0,a_i] is piecewise linear convex in δ? c' coefficients = c_k + δ d_k. Max of affine functions is convex in δ. We want minimize max; can find minimizer by ternary? Since convex, can use golden-section/ternary or derivative subgradient: derivative at δ from 1 to D differentiable; choose top lags. For a chosen j, best i is one with large value (conv(w,a))? Subgradient of LSE: h_i = 2 conv(w,a)_i. The objective change approx 2 h_i δ for source? If moving from i to j, change in weighted sum ≈ 2 h_j δ - 2 h_i δ. So choose i with large h (loss) and j with small h (gain). For exact max, weight w over top lags uniform. Compute h=2 conv(w,a) via FFT or O(n) if w sparse. For top K uniform, h_i = 2/(K)∑_{top} a_{k-i}. This can be computed directly O(nK) if K small or FFT. Coordinate descent algorithm:
- Maintain c.
- Every some iterations, recompute c via FFT to avoid drift.
- Identify top lags: top K (K increases if stuck) with largest c. Compute h_i for weights uniform over top K efficiently: For each top k, h_i += a_{k-i} valid. This is O(nK). If K up to 8, okay maybe 1e6 per iteration; too much for 100k but maybe 10k ~10^10 no. Need optimize: Use FFT to convolve w with a when K large, but top set changes. But coordinate descent should be cheap. Could use top 1 (current argmax) direct O(n). That may get stuck; use top 2/3 periodically.

- If h has many zeros, choose targets with low h.
- For each pair, compute d = 2(v_j - v_i) and find δ minimizing max(c+δ d). But v_j and v_i are length n; building d O(n) per candidate.        src_idx = argpartition(grad, top) etc

- previous if any.

Implement `run_fista(a_init, time_limit, rng, beta0, beta_rate, beta_max, step0, use_lse_backtrack=True)`:
Pseudo:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=1e3, beta_rate=1.05, beta_max=1e6, step_init=1.0, topk_fallback=4):
    n=len(a); S=1
    a=normalize(a_init,1)
    y=a.copy(); t=1
    beta=beta_init; step=step_init
    c=conv(a); best_obj=exact_obj(a); best=a.copy(); last_imp=0
    val_y, grad_y, c_y, w_y = lse_info(y,beta)
    obj_y=2*n*c_y.max()
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time-start<max_time:
       it+=1
       beta=min(beta_max, beta_init * (1.05 ** it)) # maybe exponential
       val_y, grad_y, c_y, w_y = lse_info(y,beta)
       obj_y=2*n*c_y.max()
       if obj_y<best_obj-1e-12: update best
       grad_norm=np.linalg.norm(grad_y)
       if grad_norm<1e-12: break
       # Line search on LSE or exact? Use LSE val.
       lam=step
       x=None
       inner = None
       for bt in range(8):
          x=project_simplex(y - lam*grad_y, 1.0)
          val_x,_ ,c_x,_=lse_info(x,beta)
          # Armijo: val_x <= val_y + alpha*lam*dot(grad_y, x-y)
          inner = float(np.dot(grad_y, x-y))
          if val_x <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4*lam*inner or inner > 0: # inner should negative
             break
          lam*=0.5
       # If LSE not accepted, try exact obj
       if val_x > val_y + 1e-9:
          # exact line search
          lam=step*0.5
          for bt in range(8):
             x=project_simplex(y - lam*grad_y,1.0)
             obj_x=2*n*conv(x).max()
             if obj_x <= obj_y + 1e-10:
                break
             lam*=0.5
       # FISTA update
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       z=x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       z=project? no sum maybe 1? Projection of convex combo not exactly sum1; normalize z? In FISTA, y is not required to be in feasible set but often projected. We can normalize z to sum1 and nonnegative to avoid drift.
       z=np.maximum(z,0); if sum>0 z/sum
       a_old=a; a=x; y=z; t=t_new
       # step adapt? if accepted with small lam, reduce; else increase up to limit
       if lam < step*0.5: step=lam*1.05
       else: step=min(step*1.03, 5.0)
       if it-last_imp>5000: reset t, y=a+noise
```

Fast convolution using `np.fft.rfft`:
```
def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size*=2
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
Using rfft for real; square not conjugate, correct polynomial square. For negative not allowed. Good. `irfft` returns real length size. For even size, okay.

Correlation (gradient) using full FFT:
```
def correlation_weights_a(w,a):

```
size=1<<((m+n-1)-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:m]
W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
grad=2*np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A),size)[:n]
```
But rfft of complex? W real. `A` complex. `W*np.conj(A)` complex. `irfft` expects Hermitian? Product not necessarily Hermitian, irfft of arbitrary complex gives real part? `project_simplex(v, S)`: We use S=1. Since FISTA y maybe sum not 1, projection each iteration. Optimize projection:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v)
    if np.any(v<0)? Still formula works.
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u - (css - S)/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.zeros(n)
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(css[r]-S)/(r+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Could ensure sum exactly S by adjusting max element? Floating errors small. We can normalize after if sum >0: `out *= S/out.sum()` but that may break nonneg? okay. More accuracy: projection sum almost S. To avoid drift, if out.sum()>0 out=out*(S/out.sum()). This may slightly violate constraints? no, nonnegative. Good.

`normalize(a, S=1)`: nonnegative, sum S; if sum tiny uniform. Values clip to 0.

Coordinate descent design in detail:
```
def coordinate_descent(a_init, max_time, rng, min_time=...):
    n=len(a); a=normalize(a_init,1)
    c=conv(a); best_obj=exact_obj; best=a.copy()
    it=0; last=0; K=4; step_fraction=0.25; eval_recompute=10
    while time<max_time:
       if it % 20 ==0: recompute c exact
       max_c=c.max()
       obj=2*n*max_c
       if obj<best_obj-1e-12: update; last=it
       if it-last > 20000: break
       # choose active lags: top K plus random among lags close to max
       # Use argpartition top 4*K, then select top K
       P=min(4*K, m)
       part=np.argpartition(c, -P)[-P:]
       top=part[np.argsort(c[part])[-K:]]
       # weights uniform over top; additionally if K large include random near top with c > max - 0.1*max
       # Compute grad direct:
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for k in top:
          kk=int(k)
          lo=max(0, kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1, kk)
          if hi>=lo:
             # sum a[kk - i] for i in lo..hi: a indices kk-hi .. kk-lo
             grad[lo:hi+1] += a[kk-hi:kk-lo+1][::-1]
       grad *= 2.0/K
       # source candidates: high grad and a>threshold
       R=8
       pos = a > 1e-12 * a.max()
       # temporarily set grad[neg pos] = -inf for source? use mask
       src_scores = grad.copy(); src_scores[~pos] = -np.inf
       src = np.argpartition(src_scores, -R)[-R:]
       tgt_scores = grad.copy(); tgt_scores[pos] = np.inf? We want low grad; targets can be pos or zero.
       # For target, prefer low grad and not too large a? Actually adding to large may increase others; use score grad (lower better). Maybe target_score = grad + 10*a? Not; low a and low grad. But if target a already large, adding small; okay. Use low grad.
       tgt = np.argpartition(tgt_scores, R) # R lowest; but if many -? grad nonneg.
       # generate pairs, limit to 32
       best_move=None
       for i in src:
         if a[i]<=0: continue
         for j in tgt:
            if i==j: continue
            # line search using top lags
            dvals for top? Need compute slopes at top: slope_k=2*(a[k-j] if 0<=k-j<n else 0 - a[k-i]...)
            candidates = [0, min(a_i,a_i*step_fraction), ...]
            For top P? Use top + maybe top 16.
            breakpoints; choose best over top, then exact max for best 2 candidates.
       if best_move: update a,c
       else: if stuck: add small random transfers: choose random i pos, random j, delta=a_i*0.01; recompute c.
       it+=1
```
But direct grad computation with top K: If K=4, pair generation top 8 targets low grad might include source high? okay.

Line search over top lags:
```
def best_delta_for_pair(c, top_idx, i, j, a_i, max_delta, eval_candidates=3):
    eps=1e-12
    # slopes for top idx
    slopes = np.empty(len(top))
    bases = c[top].copy()
    for t,k:
       val_j = a[k-j] if 0<=k-j<n else 0
       val_i = a[k-i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0
       slopes[t] = 2*(val_j - val_i)
    # breakpoints where line l and s equal:
    cands = np.array([eps, a_i*0.01, a_i*0.05, a_i*0.1, a_i*0.25, a_i*0.5, min(a_i,0.9), a_i-eps])
    # add breakpoints for top pairs (len<=16): if slopes diff !=0
    # choose up to 20 best over max of top lines
    # sort unique, clip [0,a_i]
    # evaluate objective approximate = max(bases + δ*slopes)
    # pick up to 3 δs with lowest approx
    # for each, compute exact max with d vector
```

We can first approximate using top slopes and only allocate d for top 1 candidate. Good.

- `conv_fft(a)`: returns c length 2n-1, nonnegative clipped.
```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size<m: size <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)
    c=c[:m]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c) # but if FFT for negative? We keep nonneg.
    return c
```
Use `A*A` not conjugate. For coefficients maybe complex due to numerical. `irfft` uses A's Hermitian squared? A*A not Hermitian; `irfft` expects Hermitian but if input not exactly, returns real? `np.fft.irfft` of a non-Hermitian complex array still computes inverse of the provided half-spectrum, effectively assuming conjugate symmetry for missing negative frequencies; this is correct for polynomial? Let's check: If A is rfft of real a, A has Hermitian symmetry for original a. A*A may not have Hermitian symmetry because square not conjugate square. The full DFT of a*a is A_full * A_full. The rfft of the result is not necessarily related to A_rfft*A_rfft? For real sequences, DFT full has symmetry X[-k]=conj(X[k]). For Y=DFT(a^2) = X^2. Then Y[-k]=X[-k]^2=conj(X[k])^2 = conj(X[k]^2)=conj(Y[k]), so Y has Hermitian symmetry. Thus the positive half Y[0:N/2] equals A_rfft^2 (elementwise) because A_rfft contains X[0:N/2]. So `irfft(A*A)` is correct. Good.

- `exact_obj(a)`:
```
def exact_obj(a):
    n=a.size
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    c=conv_fft(a)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(s*s))
```
For p sum1, exact_obj =2n max.

- `lse_conv_info(a,beta)` maybe returns c, lse_val, grad.
Need size for grad: `size=next_power_two(3*n-2?)`. Let m=2n-1. Correlation size >= m+n-1=3n-2. For n=8192, 24574 ->32768. Use `size=next_power_two(m+n-1)`.
```
def lse_info(a,beta):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    size=next_power_two(m+n-1)
    A=np.fft.fft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.ifft(A*A,size).real[:m]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    cm=c.max()
    shifted=beta*(c-cm)
    # to avoid overflow (max 0), no need; if beta huge and c-cm negative large, exp underflow okay.
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    se=e.sum()
    if se <= 0 or not finite: fallback weights at max
    else:
       w=e/se
       lse=cm + np.log(se)/beta
    grad_freq = np.fft.fft(w,size) * np.conj(A)
    grad = 2*np.fft.ifft(grad_freq,size).real[:n]
    return c,lse,grad
```
If beta very large, shifted min may be -inf, exp okay 0. If se=0 due to underflow (if c-cm <= -inf maybe cm not finite), fallback.
`np.fft.fft` of real a size maybe slower but okay. Could use rfft for c and full for grad? But c via full also. We can combine.

For topK weights and gradient in coordinate, using direct; no need.

- `normalize(a,S=1)`:
```
def normalize(a,S=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not finite or s<1e-15: return np.full(len(a), S/max(1,len(a)))
    a *= S/s
    return a
```

FISTA implementation details with top-level:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, tag, beta_init=2000.0, beta_growth=1.003, beta_max=1e6, step_init=1.0, use_lse=True, seed_offset=0):
    n=a_init.size
    a=normalize(a_init,1.0)
    y=a.copy()
    t=1.0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=exact_obj(a); best_lse=inf?
    start=time.monotonic(); last_imp=0; it=0
    step=step_init; beta=beta_init
    # precompute? no
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it +=1
       beta = min(beta_max, beta_init * (beta_growth ** it)) # could overflow; use iterative update instead
```
Avoid `beta_growth ** it` expensive; update `beta = min(beta_max, beta*beta_growth)` each iter, but need initial store. Use variable.
But if restart, keep beta.
```
       c_y, lse_y, grad_y = lse_info(y,beta)
       obj_y = 2*n*c_y.max()
       if obj_y < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj=obj_y; best_a=y.copy(); last_imp=it
       # If beta too small? no.
       grad_norm=np.linalg.norm(grad_y)
       if grad_norm < 1e-12: break
       # line search: try LSE
       lam=step
       x=None; val_x=None; obj_x=None
       for bt in range(10):
           x = project_simplex(y - lam*grad_y, 1.0)
           c_x, val_x, _ = lse_info(x,beta)
           obj_x=2*n*c_x.max()
           inner = float(np.dot(grad_y, x-y))
           # Armijo condition: val_x <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4*lam*inner
           if val_x <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4*lam*inner:
               break
           lam *=0.5
       # If not accepted in LSE, try exact obj descent with smaller step
       if val_x > val_y + 1e-9 and obj_x > obj_y + 1e-9:
           lam=step*0.5
           best_trial=None
           for bt in range(8):
               x=project_simplex(y - lam*grad_y,1.0)
               c_x=conv_fft(x) # no grad
               val_x=None
               obj_x=2*n*c_x.max()
               if obj_x <= obj_y + 1e-10:
                   best_trial=x; break
               lam*=0.5
      if best_trial is not None: x=best_trial
       # If no improvement (val_x not better and obj_x not better), maybe accept if obj_x<best_obj? We'll update best separately.
       # FISTA update
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       z = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       z = np.maximum(z,0)
       zs=z.sum()
       if zs<1e-15: z=a.copy()
       else: y=z*(1.0/zs)
       a_old=a; a=x
       t=t_new
       # step adapt: if lam < step*0.3: step=max(0.05, lam*0.9); else step=min(step*1.02, 5.0)
       if lam < step*0.4:
           step = max(0.05, lam*0.8)
       else:
           step = min(step*1.02, 5.0)
       # if no exact improve for many iterations, add noise and reset momentum
       if it - last_imp > 3000:
           t=1.0
           noise = max(1e-6, 0.002 / np.sqrt(n) * np.exp(-(it-last_imp)/3000.0))
           y = normalize(y + rng.normal(0, noise, n),1.0)
           # set a=y? To avoid discontinuity, set a=y
           a=y.copy()
       # if objective best plateau > 10000, maybe change beta? beta already
```
Issue: FISTA with normalized y after z can disrupt momentum but okay. Need `a` at update should be previous x? Standard: x_new = x, x_old = a. We set a=x after y computed. Good.

The line search condition `val_x <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4*lam*inner`: inner negative, RHS = val_y - small. But if inner = 0 due to projection no change, condition val_x<=val_y+1e-12; may accept. If x=y, no progress. Could track step. If no progress, add noise.

def lse_val_only(a,beta):
   c=conv_fft(a) (size conv 16384)
   cm=c.max(); e=exp(beta*(c-cm)); se=e.sum(); val=cm+log(se)/beta
   return c,val
```

def coordinate_descent(a_init, max_time, rng, tag="CD"):
    n=a_init.size; m=2*n-1
    a=normalize(a_init,1.0)
    c=conv_fft(a)
    best=exact_obj(a); best_a=a.copy(); start=time.monotonic(); it=0; last=0
    K=4; R=6; P=16
    while time-start<max_time:
       it+=1
       if it % 25 ==0:
          c=conv_fft(a); c_sum_check? 
       cm=c.max(); obj=2*n*cm
       if obj<best-1e-12: update; last=it
       if it-last>30000: break
       if it-last > 5000: K=min(16,K*2); P=min(64,P*2); R=8 # reduce when stuck
       else: K=4? maybe keep adaptive
       # top P indices
       all_top = np.argpartition(c, -P)[-P:]
       top = all_top[np.argsort(c[all_top])[-K:]] # these K for weights
       # maybe active idx larger P for line search approx
       # compute grad from K weights direct
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for k in top:
           kk=int(k)
           lo=max(0, kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1,kk)
           if hi>=lo:
              grad[lo:hi+1] += a[kk-hi:kk-lo+1][::-1]
       grad *= 2.0 / max(1,len(top))
       # source high grad positive
       pos = a > 1e-14
       src_scores=grad.copy(); src_scores[~pos]= -np.inf
       if np.all(~pos): break
       rs=min(R,n)
       src=np.argpartition(src_scores, -rs)[-rs:]
       tgt=np.argpartition(grad, R)[:R] # low grad; if all inf? no
       # exclude targets equal source? We'll
       best_move_delta=inf? Actually accept if obj improves best/current.
       improved=False
       # candidate pairs: prioritize highest improvement approx. Compute approx for each pair using all_top P.
       pair_cands=[]
       for i in src:
          if a[i]<=0: continue
          i=int(i)
          # maybe for each j
          for j in tgt:
             j=int(j); 
             if i==j: continue
             # slopes at all_top
             # Use clip for index:
             # val_j = a[k-j] if 0<=k-j<n else 0. We can precompute for i and j? For each k, need two random accesses. Loop P.
             # approx max over all_top
             best_approx=inf; best_delta_approx=0
             # We can evaluate a small set of δ fractions and breakpoints? Simpler: evaluate a few δ fractions (1,5,10,25,50,90% of a_i) and pick min approx.
             a_i=a[i]
             deltas = np.unique(np.clip([a_i*0.02, a_i*0.05, a_i*0.1, a_i*0.25, a_i*0.5, a_i*0.8, a_i],0,a_i))
             for delta in deltas:

                # Could vectorize:
                # idx_j = k - j; valid 0<=idx_j<n -> a_jk array
                kj=all_top - j
                kj_i=all_top - i
                vj=np.where((kj>=0)&(kj<n), a[kj[(kj>=0)&(kj<n)]], 0.0)
                vi=np.where((ki>=0)&(ki<n), a[ki[(ki>=0)&(ki<n)]], 0.0)
                slopes=2*(vj-vi)
                vals=c[all_top] + delta*slopes
                app=vals.max()
                if app<best_approx: best_approx=app; best_delta=delta; also store slopes? 
             if best_delta>0 and best_approx < cm - 1e-12: # potential
                pair_cands.append((best_approx, i,j,best_delta))
       if pair_cands:
          pair_cands.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])  # lambda not allowed! Need top-level function? We can sort by list and use `pair_cands.sort()` if tuple first. Tuples sort by first. No lambda.
          # Try top 3
          for approx,i,j,delta in pair_cands[:3]:
              # exact check with d
              d = np.zeros(m)
              d[j:j+n] += a
              d[i:i+n] -= a
              d *= 2.0
              newc = c + delta*d
              new_obj = 2*n*newc.max()
              if new_obj < best - 1e-12 or new_obj < obj - 1e-12:
                  a[i] -= delta; a[j] += delta; c = newc
                  if a[i] <0 tiny: a[i]=0
                  improved=True
                  break
       if not improved:

             i = rng.choice(np.where(pos)[0]) if any pos

                if delta>1e-15:
                    a[i]-=delta; a[j]+=delta; c=conv_fft(a)

for _ in range(10):
    x=project(y-lm*grad)
    c_x,val_x=lse_val_only(x,beta); obj_x=2*n*c_x.max()
    inner=dot(grad,x-y)

       step *=0.5

```
def make_random_inits(n, rng, count=20):
    inits=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    # known
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    alphas = np.array([0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9])
    for alpha in alphas:
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*(1-2*x),1e-8,None); inits.append(a)
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*(2*x-1),1e-8,None); inits.append(a)
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x),1e-8,None); inits.append(a)
       a=np.clip(1-alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x),1e-8,None); inits.append(a)
       a=np.clip(1+alpha*np.cos(3*np.pi*x),1e-8,None); inits.append(a)
    exps=[]
    for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0,4.0]:
       r=np.exp(-lam/n)
       idx=np.arange(n)
       a=np.maximum(r**idx,1e-8); inits.append(a)
       a=np.maximum(r**idx[::-1],1e-8); inits.append(a)
    for _ in range(count):
       # random anti-palindromic cos
       c0=rng.uniform(-1,1)*0.6; c1=rng.uniform(-1,1)*0.3; c2=rng.uniform(-1,1)*0.15
       a=1 + c0*np.cos(np.pi*x) + c1*np.cos(3*np.pi*x) + c2*np.cos(5*np.pi*x)
       # random smooth no anti
       z=rng.normal(0,1,n)
       kernel=np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-20,21)/5.0)**2); kernel/=kernel.sum()
       zs=np.convolve(z,kernel,'same')
       zs=(zs-zs.mean())/ (zs.std()+1e-6)
       a2=1+0.4*zs
       a=np.clip(a + 0.3*a2, 1e-8, None)
       inits.append(a)
    return inits
```
Could be many inits; okay. For each, normalize sum1 before obj.

`screen_candidates(inits, n, max_count=8)`:

def screen_inits(inits):
   res=[]
   for a in inits:
      a=normalize(a,1.0)
      obj=exact_obj(a)
      if finite: res.append((obj,a))
   res.sort() # tuple sort by obj
   return res[:8]
```

Upsample:
```
def upsample(a, factor=2):
    n=len(a); n2=n*factor
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n2,endpoint=False)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return b
```

Top-level `finalize_sequence(a)`:
```
def finalize_sequence(a, budget_left=None):
    if a is None:
       n=8192; a=np.ones(n)/n
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if not finite or s<1e-15: uniform
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*len(a))
    a = a * (S / s)
    # ensure no NaN/inf, values finite
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    # if sum after scale <0.01? S >=? for n>=1 S>=1.4
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```

Coordinate d update: For source i, target j. c' = c + 2δ(v_j - v_i)? Let's verify with n=2, a=[a0,a1], transfer δ from i=0 to j=1: a'=[a0-δ,a1+δ]. c0'=(a0-δ)^2=c0-2δa0+δ^2. Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_power_two(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    p=1
    while p<n:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```

`conv_fft`:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    size=next_power_two(m)
    af=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(af*af,size)
    c=c[:m]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c
```
For a length maybe 1, m=1.

`exact_objective`:
```
def exact_objective(a):
    n=a.size
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c=conv_fft(a)
    cm=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(cm): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cm/(s*s))
```

`lse_value_only`:
```
def lse_value_only(a,beta):
    c=conv_fft(a)
    cm=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return c, float(cm)
    beta=float(beta)
    shifted=beta*(c-cm)
    # To avoid all zeros? max shifted 0.
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    se=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        val=float(cm)
    else:
        val=float(cm + np.log(se)/beta)
    return c,val
```
If beta huge and se inf? shifted max0, min negative, exp no inf. If beta nan? no.

`lse_value_gradient`:
```
def lse_value_gradient(a,beta):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    size=next_power_two(m+n-1)
    af=np.fft.fft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.ifft(af*af,size).real[:m]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0); c=np.nan_to_num(...)
    cm=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0:
        return c,float(cm),np.zeros(n)
    shifted=beta*(c-cm)
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    se=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        w=np.zeros(m); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; val=float(cm)
    else:
        w=e/se; val=float(cm + np.log(se)/beta)
    wf=np.fft.fft(w,size)
    grad=np.fft.ifft(wf*np.conj(af),size).real[:n]
    grad=2.0*grad
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c,val,grad
```

`project_simplex` as above.

`normalize_nonnegative`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a,S=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy().reshape(-1)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0000000001? maybe 0.0,1000.0)
```
Should we clip lower to 0 only, not tiny. If we add tiny, sum changes? okay. For FISTA, zeros allowed. Use `np.maximum(a,0)`.
If not finite values, set 0.
If s < 1e-15: uniform.
Return `a * (S/s)`. To ensure finite, `np.nan_to_num`.

```
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        a=np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s=max(1,n)
    a *= S/s
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    return a
```

        a=np.maximum(1.0 + alpha*(2.0*x -1.0), 1e-8); res.append(a)

Exponential:

       r=np.exp(-lam/max(1,n))

Random smooth count:
```
    ker_len=21
    ker_idx=np.arange(ker_len)-10
    kern=np.exp(-0.5*(ker_idx/4.0)**2); kern/=kern.sum()
    for _ in range(count):
        c0=rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8); c1=rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4); c2=rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2)
        a=1.0 + c0*np.cos(np.pi*x) + c1*np.cos(3.0*np.pi*x) + c2*np.cos(5.0*np.pi*x)
        z=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
        zs=np.convolve(z,kern,'same')
        zs = zs - zs.mean()
        sd=zs.std()
        if sd > 1e-8: zs *= 0.4/sd
        a = a + 0.5*zs
        a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
        res.append(a)
    return res

This may produce a with very low values; normalize clips.

`screen_inits`:
```
def screen_key(item): return item[0]
def screen_inits(inits, max_count=6):
    out=[]
    for a in inits:
        b=normalize_nonnegative(a,1.0)
        if b.size == 0: continue
        o=exact_objective(b)
        if np.isfinite(o):
            out.append((o,b))
    out.sort(key=screen_key)
    if max_count is None: return out
    return out[:max_count]
```
Need `screen_key` top-level. Good.

`upsample`:
```
def upsample(a, factor=2):
    n=a.size
    factor=int(max(1,factor))
    n2=n*factor
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n2,endpoint=False)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return b
```

`run_fista` detailed code:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, tag="fista", beta_init=5000.0, beta_growth=1.002, beta_max=1000000.0, step_init=1.0, step_max=3.0):
    n=a_init.size
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a_init,1.0)
    if max_time <= 0:
        return a, exact_objective(a)
    y=a.copy()
    t=1.0
    beta=float(beta_init)
    step=float(step_init)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_obj=exact_objective(a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    last_imp=0
    it=0
    no_move_count=0
    print("  [%s] start n=%d obj=%.7f" % (tag,n,best_obj))
    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        # update beta
        if beta_growth != 1.0:
            beta *= beta_growth
            if beta > beta_max: beta = beta_max
        c_y, val_y, grad = lse_value_gradient(y,beta)
        obj_y = float(2.0*n*c_y.max())
        if obj_y < best_obj - 1e-13:
            best_obj=obj_y; best_a=y.copy(); last_imp=it
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-13:
            break
        lam = step
        x = None
        accepted = False
        best_backup = None
        best_backup_score = np.inf
        for bt in range(10):
            x_trial = project_simplex(y - lam*grad, 1.0)
            c_trial, val_trial = lse_value_only(x_trial, beta)
            obj_trial = float(2.0*n*c_trial.max())
            inner = float(np.dot(grad, x_trial - y))
            if inner < 0.0:
                if val_trial <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4*lam*inner:
                    x = x_trial
                    accepted = True
                    break
            else:
                # No descent in projected direction; if very small, accept to stop
                if np.linalg.norm(x_trial - y) < 1e-12 * max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(y)):
                    x = x_trial
                    accepted = True
                    break
            score = val_trial + 1e-6*obj_trial
            if score < best_backup_score:
                best_backup_score = score
                best_backup = x_trial
            lam *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            if best_backup is not None:
                ok = False
                # evaluate backup objective
                c_bk = conv_fft(best_backup) # or if we have? best_backup from earlier, didn't store obj. Just use val? Need exact maybe.
                obj_bk = 2.0*n*c_bk.max()
                if obj_bk <= obj_y + 1e-9 and val_y? can't val. But val not stored. skip maybe accept if obj_bk < best_obj?
                if obj_bk < best_obj - 1e-12:
                    x = best_backup; accepted=True
            if not accepted:
                # no move
                x = y.copy()
                step = max(0.05, step*0.5)
                t = 1.0
                no_move_count += 1
            else:
                no_move_count = 0
        else:
            no_move_count = 0
            # update best from x
            # c_trial maybe not stored? We didn't keep c_trial outside. We can compute objective for x if accepted in loop.
```
We need store accepted candidate's objective. In loop when accepted, we have `obj_trial`. After loop, if accepted, do:
```
obj_x = obj_trial
if obj_x < best_obj - 1e-13: best_obj=obj_x; best_a=x.copy(); last_imp=it
```
But if accepted in inner >=0 branch, no obj? set obj_trial? We evaluate before branch. Good.
Need handle `x` from backup where we didn't compute val/obj? We can compute obj_bk and if improve best; set x.

Step adapt: If accepted and lam < step*0.3 -> step=max(0.05, lam*0.8); else step=min(step*1.02, step_max). If no move, step reduced.

FISTA update:
```
        t_new = 0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
        # if x is None
        if x is None: x=y.copy()
        z = x + ((t - 1.0)/t_new)*(x - a)
        z = np.maximum(z,0.0)
        zs=float(z.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(zs) or zs < 1e-15:
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            y = z*(1.0/zs)
        a = x
        t = t_new
        if it - last_imp > 4000 and it % 100 == 0:
            t = 1.0
            ns = max(1e-8, 0.002/np.sqrt(n))
            y = normalize_nonnegative(y + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n), 1.0)
            a = y.copy()
        if it % 200 == 0:
            print("  [%s] it=%d obj=%.7f beta=%.3e step=%.4f" % (tag,it,best_obj,beta,step))
    # final best exact
    obj_best = exact_objective(best_a)
    if obj_best < best_obj: best_obj=obj_best
    return best_a, best_obj
```
But note `a` used in momentum is previous x. We update after x. If no move x=y, a becomes y (same as previous y) may break momentum; okay.

Potential inefficiency: In no accepted branch, `conv_fft(best_backup)` recomputes; we could store backup obj. Modify loop store `best_backup = (x_trial, val_trial, obj_trial)`. Then no recompute. Let's do.
```
best_backup=None
...
if score<...: best_backup=(x_trial,val_trial,obj_trial)
...
if best_backup is not None:
    x_b,v_b,o_b=best_backup
    if o_b <= obj_y + 1e-9:
       x=x_b; accepted=True; obj_x=o_b; last_improve if...
```
Good.

However if accepted via Armijo, we need `obj_x`. Use variable `obj_x = obj_trial` before break.

Inner gradient dot may be expensive O(n), okay.

`lse_value_gradient` and `lse_value_only` both compute conv, so each iteration at least 2 FFTs. If no accepted and backup from loop, maybe up to 10 trials, heavy. Backtracking few.

Could limit line search trials to 6 for speed. Use 8.

`run_coordinate_descent` detailed code with exact quadratic update:
```
def run_coordinate_descent(a_init, max_time, rng, tag="CD"):
    n=a_init.size
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a_init,1.0)
    if max_time<=0: return a, exact_objective(a)
    m=2*n-1
    c=conv_fft(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=2.0*n*c.max()
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0; last=0; moves=0
    K=3; P=8; R=5
    print("  [%s] start n=%d obj=%.7f" % (tag,n,best_obj))
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
        it+=1
        if it % 50 == 0:
            c=conv_fft(a)
            # also normalize a to sum1? Since c from a; if sum drifted, exact obj scale? We can renormalize a and c:
            sa=a.sum()
            if sa>0 and abs(sa-1)>1e-10:
                a*=1.0/sa
                c*=1.0/(sa*sa)
        cm=float(c.max()); obj=2*n*cm
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
            best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last=it
        if it - last > 30000:
            break
        stuck = it - last
        # adjust K,P,R
        if stuck < 1000:
            K=3; P=8; R=4
        elif stuck < 5000:
            K=5; P=14; R=5
        elif stuck < 15000:
            K=8; P=24; R=6
        else:
            K=12; P=40; R=7
        # top P
        Puse=min(P, m)
        top_all = np.argpartition(c, -Puse)[-Puse:]
        # sort top_all by c descending
        top_all = top_all[np.argsort(c[top_all])[::-1]]
        top = top_all[:min(K, m)]
        # grad direct
        grad=np.zeros(n)
        for kk in top:
            k=int(k)
            lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
            if hi >= lo:
                grad[lo:hi+1] += a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
        grad *= 2.0 / max(1, top.size)
        pos = a > 1e-14
        if not np.any(pos): break
        rs=R
        src_scores=grad.copy()
        src_scores[~pos] = -np.inf
        if rs > n: rs = n
        # To avoid all -inf? skip
        src = np.argpartition(src_scores, -rs)[-rs:]
        tgt = np.argpartition(grad, min(rs, n))[:rs] # if rs=0?
        # Candidate pairs approx
        c_top = c[top_all] + 0.0 # length Puse
        pair_cands=[]
        for ii in src:
            i=int(ii)
            ai=a[i]
            if ai <= 1e-15: continue
            for jj in tgt:
                j=int(jj)
                if i == j: continue
                kj = top_all - j
                ki = top_all - i
                vj = np.zeros(Puse)
                mask = (kj >= 0) & (kj < n)
                vj[mask] = a[kj[mask]]
                vi = np.zeros(Puse)
                mask_i = (ki >= 0) & (ki < n)
                vi[mask_i] = a[ki[mask_i]]
                slopes = 2.0 * (vj - vi)
                # q_top quadratic
                q = np.zeros(Puse)
                # vectorized comparisons
                q += (top_all == 2*i).astype(float)
                q += (top_all == 2*j).astype(float)
                q -= 2.0 * (top_all == (i+j)).astype(float)
                # candidate deltas
                deltas = np.array([ai*0.01, ai*0.03, ai*0.1, ai*0.3, ai*0.6, ai*0.9, ai], dtype=np.float64)
                deltas = np.clip(deltas, 0.0, ai)
                deltas = np.unique(deltas)
                d2 = deltas*deltas
                vals = c_top[None,:] + deltas[:,None]*slopes[None,:] + d2[:,None]*q[None,:]
                approx = vals.max(axis=1)
                # pick best approx
                ib = int(np.argmin(approx))
                if approx[ib] < cm - 1e-12:
                    pair_cands.append((float(approx[ib]), i, j, float(deltas[ib])))
        improved=False
        if pair_cands:
            pair_cands.sort()
            # try up to 3
            for _, i, j, delta in pair_cands[:3]:
                ai=a[i]
                if delta <= 0 or delta > ai + 1e-15:
                    continue
                delta = min(delta, ai)
                d=np.zeros(m)
                d[j:j+n] += a
                d[i:i+n] -= a
                d *= 2.0
                # Instead of allocating q full, update newc with in-place indices
                newc = c + delta*d
                delta2 = delta*delta
                if 2*i < m: newc[2*i] += delta2
                if 2*j < m: newc[2*j] += delta2
                if i+j < m: newc[i+j] -= 2.0*delta2
                # ensure nonneg? newc may tiny negative
                new_obj = 2.0*n*float(newc.max())
                if new_obj < best_obj - 1e-13 or new_obj < obj - 1e-12:
                    a[i] = ai - delta
                    if a[i] < 0: a[i] = 0.0
                    a[j] += delta
                    c = newc
                    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
                    moves += 1
                    improved=True
                    break
        if not improved:
            if stuck > 800 or (it % 500 == 0):
                pos_idx=np.nonzero(pos)[0]
                if pos_idx.size > 0:
                    i=int(pos_idx[rng.integers(0,pos_idx.size)])
                    ai=a[i]
                    if ai > 1e-15:
                        j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
                        if i != j:
                            delta=ai * float(rng.uniform(0.0005,0.005))
                            d=np.zeros(m)
                            d[j:j+n] += a
                            d[i:i+n] -= a
                            d *= 2.0
                            newc = c + delta*d
                            delta2=delta*delta
                            newc[2*i] += delta2
                            newc[2*j] += delta2
                            newc[i+j] -= 2.0*delta2
                            a[i]-=delta; a[j]+=delta
                            c=np.maximum(newc,0.0)
        if it % 200 == 0:
            print("  [%s] it=%d obj=%.7f best=%.7f" % (tag,it,obj,best_obj))
    # final
    obj_best=exact_objective(best_a)
    if obj_best < best_obj: best_obj=obj_best
    return best_a, best_obj
```
Need be careful: `newc[c]` from c+delta*d uses previous `a` linear term; then quadratic exact. But if we update `a` before c? We computed d with old a. Good.
`newc[i+j]` maybe repeated if 2i or 2j equal i+j? For distinct i,j, i+j equals 2i only if j=i; not. Could equal 2j if i=j. So okay. But if indices beyond m? 2i up to 2n-2=m-1, 2j, i+j ≤2n-2. Good.

`pair_cands.sort()` sorts by approx, i,j,delta. okay.

Potential memory/time: In coordinate, for each pair we allocate `vj`, `vi`, `q`, `deltas`, `vals`. R=7 =>49 pairs, P=40, D=7 -> vals 7x40, small. But allocations per pair many. Could be overhead but okay. 10k*49 ~490k small arrays; may be heavy but okay in Python. To reduce, R=5 initially. We can keep.

If `n` large and `m` large, `d=np.zeros(m)` allocation for accepted only. Good.

But `pos = a > 1e-14` each iter; if many zeros, pos_idx update not stored for random? We compute.

`np.argpartition(grad, min(rs,n))` returns `rs` smallest, but if rs = n okay. For `src_scores` with -inf, `argpartition` may put -inf at end; selecting last rs gives top finite. If all -inf (no pos) break; okay.

Potential bug: `tgt = np.argpartition(grad, min(rs, n))[:rs]` selects rs smallest values but if rs = n, partitions whole array; okay. If rs=0? R≥4. For small n, rs=n -> top and bottom same; pairs skip. If n<2, not.

Now, final `propose_candidate` code. Need manage top-level no nested. Let's write with prints.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except Exception: budget=2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0: budget=2400.0
    budget=min(budget,2400.0)
    deadline=start+max(60.0, budget-60.0)  # leave 60s
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_obj=np.inf
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is not None:
       try:
          ha=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

```
def finalize_sequence(a):
    if a is None:
        n=8192
        a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    n=a.size
    if n==0:
       a=np.ones(8192); n=8192; s=8192
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-15:
       a=np.ones(n)/max(1,n); s=1.0
    target=np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    a=a*(target/s)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```

```
def upsample(a,factor):
    n=a.size; n2=n*factor
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n; x_new=(np.arange(n2)+0.5)/n2
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2) for non-negative a.

The code focuses on the scale-invariant problem: set sum(a)=1 and minimize
2*n*max(a*a).  It uses:
  - diverse analytic / smooth initializations,
  - accelerated projected gradient / FISTA with log-sum-exp smoothing of the
    convolution coefficients,
  - exact O(n) coordinate transport moves with quadratic update of the
    convolution,
  - optional up-sampling to larger lengths near the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_power_two(n):
    """Smallest power of two >= n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def conv_fft(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution of a real array via FFT."""
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_two(m)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(af * af, size)
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c


def objective_from_conv(a, c):
    """Objective 2*n*max(c)/sum(a)^2 for a given convolution c."""
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def exact_objective(a):
    """Objective computed from the exact FFT convolution."""
    return objective_from_conv(a, conv_fft(a))


def lse_value_only(a, beta):
    """Return convolution and smooth log-sum-exp surrogate for max convolution."""
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return c, float(cm)

    beta = float(beta)
    shifted = beta * (c - cm)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        val = float(cm)
    else:
        val = float(cm + np.log(se) / beta)
    return c, val


def lse_value_gradient(a, beta):
    """
    Return (c, lse_value, gradient of lse_value w.r.t. a).

    For c_k = a*a coefficient, derivative is 2 * correlation(weights, a)_i.
    """
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_two(m + n - 1)

    af = np.fft.fft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.ifft(af * af, size).real[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return c, float(cm), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    shifted = beta * (c - cm)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    se = float(e.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        val = float(cm)
    else:
        w = e / se
        val = float(cm + np.log(se) / beta)

    wf = np.fft.fft(w, size)
    grad = np.fft.ifft(wf * np.conj(af), size).real[:n]
    grad = 2.0 * grad
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, val, grad


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    valid = u - (css - S) / idx > 0.0
    rho = np.nonzero(valid)[0]

    if rho.size == 0:
        out = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return out

    r = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (css[r] - S) / float(r + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        out = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out *= S / ss
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_nonnegative(a, S=1.0):
    """Clip to non-negative and rescale to sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(max(1, a.size), S / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(S) / s)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    return a


def resize_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Interpolate a to length n_new using centers instead of endpoints."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n_old = a.size
    if n_old == int(n_new):
        return a.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(int(n_new), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(int(n_new))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def upsample(a, factor=2):
    """Linear up-sampling by integer factor."""
    factor = int(max(1, factor))
    n = a.size
    n_new = n * factor
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def obj_item_key(item):
    """Sort key for (objective, array) pairs."""
    return item[0]


def screen_inits(inits, max_count=6):
    """Normalize and rank initial arrays by exact objective."""
    out = []
    for a in inits:
        b = normalize_nonnegative(a, 1.0)
        if b.size == 0:
            continue
        o = exact_objective(b)
        if np.isfinite(o):
            out.append((o, b))
    out.sort(key=obj_item_key)
    if max_count is None:
        return out
    return out[:max_count]


def make_random_inits(n, rng, count=12):
    """Create a pool of low-dimensional / smooth non-negative initial shapes."""
    res = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return res

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    res.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    alphas = np.array([0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9], dtype=np.float64)
    for alpha in alphas:
        a = np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - 2.0 * x), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

        a = np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

        a = np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(np.pi * x), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

        a = np.maximum(1.0 - alpha * np.cos(np.pi * x), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

        a = np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

        a = np.maximum(1.0 - alpha * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for lam in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0]:
        r = float(np.exp(-lam / float(n)))
        a = np.maximum(r ** idx, 1e-8)
        res.append(a)
        a = np.maximum(r ** idx[::-1], 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

    ker_len = 21
    ker_idx = np.arange(ker_len, dtype=np.float64) - 10.0
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (ker_idx / 4.0) ** 2)
    kern = kern / kern.sum()

    for _ in range(int(count)):
        c0 = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8))
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.4))
        c2 = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2))
        a = 1.0 + c0 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + c1 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x) + c2 * np.cos(5.0 * np.pi * x)

        z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        zs = np.convolve(z, kern, "same")
        zs = zs - float(zs.mean())
        sd = float(zs.std())
        if sd > 1e-8:
            zs *= 0.4 / sd
        a = a + 0.5 * zs
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        res.append(a)

    return res


def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, tag="fista",
              beta_init=5000.0, beta_growth=1.002, beta_max=2000000.0,
              step_init=1.0, step_max=3.0):
    """
    Accelerated projected gradient method for the LSE-smoothed objective.

    The returned sequence is the best exact objective seen during the run.
    """
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a_init.size
    if n == 0:
        return a_init, np.inf

    a = normalize_nonnegative(a_init, 1.0)
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return a, exact_objective(a)

    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    beta = float(beta_init)
    step = float(step_init)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = exact_objective(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    last_imp = 0
    it = 0

    print("  [%s] start n=%d obj=%.8f" % (tag, n, best_obj))

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1

        if beta_growth != 1.0:
            beta *= float(beta_growth)
            if beta > beta_max:
                beta = float(beta_max)

        c_y, val_y, grad = lse_value_gradient(y, beta)
        s_y = float(y.sum())
        if s_y <= 1e-15:
            break
        obj_y = float(2.0 * n * c_y.max() / (s_y * s_y))

        if obj_y < best_obj - 1e-13:
            best_obj = obj_y
            best_a = y.copy()
            last_imp = it

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-13:
            break

        lam = step
        x = None
        obj_x = obj_y
        accepted = False
        backup = None

        for _ in range(8):
            x_trial = project_simplex(y - lam * grad, 1.0)
            c_trial, val_trial = lse_value_only(x_trial, beta)
            s_trial = float(x_trial.sum())
            if s_trial <= 1e-15:
                lam *= 0.5
                continue
            obj_trial = float(2.0 * n * c_trial.max() / (s_trial * s_trial))
            inner = float(np.dot(grad, x_trial - y))

            if inner < 0.0:
                if val_trial <= val_y + 1e-12 + 1e-4 * lam * inner:
                    x = x_trial
                    obj_x = obj_trial
                    accepted = True
                    break
            else:
                diff = x_trial - y
                if float(np.linalg.norm(diff)) < 1e-12:
                    x = x_trial
                    obj_x = obj_trial
                    accepted = True
                    break

            score = val_trial + 1e-6 * obj_trial
            if backup is None or score < backup[0]:
                backup = (score, x_trial, val_trial, obj_trial)

            lam *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            if backup is not None and backup[3] <= obj_y + 1e-9:
                x = backup[1]
                obj_x = backup[3]
                accepted = True
            else:
                x = y.copy()
                obj_x = obj_y
                step = max(0.05, step * 0.5)
                t = 1.0
        else:
            if obj_x < best_obj - 1e-13:
                best_obj = obj_x
                best_a = x.copy()
                last_imp = it

            if lam < step * 0.3:
                step = max(0.05, lam * 0.8)
            else:
                step = min(step * 1.02, step_max)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        z = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
        zs = float(z.sum())
        if zs <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(zs):
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            y = z * (1.0 / zs)

        a = x
        t = t_new

        if it - last_imp > 4000 and it % 100 == 0:
            t = 1.0
            ns = max(1e-8, 0.002 / np.sqrt(float(n)))
            y = normalize_nonnegative(y + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n), 1.0)
            a = y.copy()

        if it % 300 == 0:
            print("  [%s] it=%d obj=%.8f beta=%.3e step=%.4f" % (tag, it, best_obj, beta, step))

    final_obj = exact_objective(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_obj) and final_obj < best_obj:
        best_obj = final_obj
    return best_a, best_obj


def run_coordinate_descent(a_init, max_time, rng, tag="CD"):
    """
    Exact coordinate transport descent.

    A move transfers mass from i to j.  The convolution update uses the exact
    linear and quadratic terms, so c remains an exact convolution between
    periodic re-computations.
    """
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a_init.size
    if n == 0:
        return a_init, np.inf

    a = normalize_nonnegative(a_init, 1.0)
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return a, exact_objective(a)

    m = 2 * n - 1
    c = conv_fft(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    best_obj = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    last_imp = 0

    print("  [%s] start n=%d obj=%.8f" % (tag, n, best_obj))

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1

        if it % 50 == 0:
            c = conv_fft(a)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-10:
                a = a * (1.0 / s)
                c = c * (1.0 / (s * s))

        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            break
        cm = float(c.max())
        obj = float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))

        if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_imp = it

        if it - last_imp > 30000:
            break

        stuck = it - last_imp
        if stuck < 1000:
            K, P, R = 3, 8, 4
        elif stuck < 5000:
            K, P, R = 5, 12, 5
        elif stuck < 15000:
            K, P, R = 8, 20, 6
        else:
            K, P, R = 12, 32, 7

        Puse = int(min(P, m))
        top_all = np.argpartition(c, -Puse)[-Puse:]
        top_all = top_all[np.argsort(c[top_all])[::-1]]
        top = top_all[:int(min(K, m))]

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in top:
            k = int(kk)
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if hi >= lo:
                grad[lo:hi + 1] += a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
        grad *= 2.0 / max(1, top.size)

        pos = a > 1e-14
        if not np.any(pos):
            break

        Ruse = int(min(R, n))
        src_scores = grad.copy()
        src_scores[~pos] = -np.inf
        if Ruse > 0:
            src = np.argpartition(src_scores, -Ruse)[-Ruse:]
        else:
            src = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        tgt = np.argpartition(grad, Ruse)[:Ruse]

        c_top = c[top_all].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        pair_cands = []

        for ii in src:
            i = int(ii)
            ai = float(a[i])
            if ai <= 1e-15:
                continue

            for jj in tgt:
                j = int(jj)
                if i == j:
                    continue

                kj = top_all - j
                ki = top_all - i

                vj = np.zeros(Puse, dtype=np.float64)
                mj = (kj >= 0) & (kj < n)
                vj[mj] = a[kj[mj]]

                vi = np.zeros(Puse, dtype=np.float64)
                mi = (ki >= 0) & (ki < n)
                vi[mi] = a[ki[mi]]

                slopes = 2.0 * (vj - vi)

                q = np.zeros(Puse, dtype=np.float64)
                q += (top_all == 2 * i).astype(np.float64)
                q += (top_all == 2 * j).astype(np.float64)
                q -= 2.0 * (top_all == (i + j)).astype(np.float64)

                deltas = ai * np.array([0.02, 0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 0.80, 1.00], dtype=np.float64)
                deltas = np.clip(deltas, 0.0, ai)
                deltas = np.unique(deltas)

                vals = (
                    c_top[None, :]
                    + deltas[:, None] * slopes[None, :]
                    + (deltas * deltas)[:, None] * q[None, :]
                )
                approx = vals.max(axis=1)
                ib = int(np.argmin(approx))

                if approx[ib] < cm - 1e-12:
                    pair_cands.append((float(approx[ib]), i, j, float(deltas[ib])))

        improved = False
        if pair_cands:
            pair_cands.sort()
            for _, i, j, delta in pair_cands[:3]:
                ai = float(a[i])
                if ai <= 1e-15 or delta <= 0.0:
                    continue
                delta = min(delta, ai)

                d = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                d[j:j + n] += a
                d[i:i + n] -= a
                d *= 2.0

                newc = c + delta * d
                delta2 = delta * delta
                newc[2 * i] += delta2
                newc[2 * j] += delta2
                newc[i + j] -= 2.0 * delta2

                newc = np.maximum(newc, 0.0)
                new_obj = float(2.0 * n * newc.max() / (s * s))

                if new_obj < best_obj - 1e-13 or new_obj < obj - 1e-12:
                    a[i] = ai - delta
                    if a[i] < 0.0:
                        a[i] = 0.0
                    a[j] += delta
                    c = newc
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved and (stuck > 800 or it % 500 == 0):
            pos_idx = np.nonzero(pos)[0]
            if pos_idx.size > 0:
                i = int(pos_idx[int(rng.integers(0, pos_idx.size))])
                ai = float(a[i])
                if ai > 1e-15:
                    j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                    if i != j:
                        delta = ai * float(rng.uniform(0.0005, 0.005))
                        d = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                        d[j:j + n] += a
                        d[i:i + n] -= a
                        d *= 2.0

                        newc = c + delta * d
                        delta2 = delta * delta
                        newc[2 * i] += delta2
                        newc[2 * j] += delta2
                        newc[i + j] -= 2.0 * delta2

                        a[i] -= delta
                        a[j] += delta
                        c = np.maximum(newc, 0.0)

        if it % 500 == 0:
            print("  [%s] it=%d obj=%.8f best=%.8f" % (tag, it, obj, best_obj))

    final_obj = exact_objective(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_obj) and final_obj < best_obj:
        best_obj = final_obj
    return best_a, best_obj


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid Python list scaled to sum sqrt(2*n)."""
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        s = 1.0

    target = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    a = a * (target / s)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation function."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(budget, 2400.0)

    if budget > 180.0:
        reserve = 60.0
    elif budget > 60.0:
        reserve = 30.0
    else:
        reserve = max(2.0, 0.1 * budget)
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = None
    best_obj = np.inf

    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            ha = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            ha = np.nan_to_num(ha, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            ha = np.clip(ha, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if ha.size >= 2 and float(ha.sum()) > 0.01:
                a_h = normalize_nonnegative(ha, 1.0)
                o_h = exact_objective(a_h)
                if np.isfinite(o_h):
                    best_a = a_h
                    best_obj = o_h
                    print("Loaded height_sequence_1: n=%d obj=%.8f" % (a_h.size, best_obj))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("Could not load height_sequence_1:", exc)

    n_target = 8192
    if best_a is not None and 4096 <= best_a.size <= 16384:
        n_target = best_a.size

    if best_a is not None:
        best_a = normalize_nonnegative(resize_sequence(best_a, n_target), 1.0)
        best_obj = exact_objective(best_a)
        print("Resized/normalized reference: n=%d obj=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_obj))

    print("\n=== Initial screening (n=%d) ===" % n_target)
    inits = make_random_inits(n_target, rng, count=14)
    if best_a is not None:
        inits.append(best_a)

    # Coarse pre-optimization when no strong reference is available.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if (best_a is None or best_obj > 1.55) and remaining > 300:
        n_coarse = 1024
        coarse = make_random_inits(n_coarse, rng, count=10)
        if best_a is not None:
            coarse.append(resize_sequence(best_a, n_coarse))
        cs = screen_inits(coarse, max_count=4)
        if cs:
            t_co = min(120.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
            if t_co > 30:
                a_co, o_co = run_fista(
                    cs[0][1], t_co, rng,
                    tag="coarse",
                    beta_init=2000.0,
                    beta_growth=1.004,
                    beta_max=100000.0,
                    step_init=1.0,
                    step_max=3.0
                )
                a_cu = normalize_nonnegative(resize_sequence(a_co, n_target), 1.0)
                inits.append(a_cu)

    screened = screen_inits(inits, max_count=8)
    print("Top initial objective: %.8f" % (screened[0][0] if screened else best_obj))

    for o, a in screened:
        if o < best_obj - 1e-14:
            best_obj = o
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Phase 1: primary FISTA.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if screened and remaining > 60:
        t1 = min(1000.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.38))
        if t1 > 30:
            a1, o1 = run_fista(
                screened[0][1], t1, rng,
                tag="main",
                beta_init=5000.0,
                beta_growth=1.002,
                beta_max=2000000.0,
                step_init=1.0,
                step_max=3.0
            )
            if o1 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o1
                best_a = a1.copy()

    # Phase 2: smoother / different landscape.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if screened and remaining > 60:
        second = screened[1][1] if len(screened) > 1 else best_a
        t2 = min(500.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.20))
        if t2 > 30:
            a2, o2 = run_fista(
                second, t2, rng,
                tag="smooth",
                beta_init=500.0,
                beta_growth=1.003,
                beta_max=100000.0,
                step_init=1.5,
                step_max=4.0
            )
            if o2 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o2
                best_a = a2.copy()

    # Phase 3: high-beta sharp FISTA.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if screened and len(screened) > 2 and remaining > 60:
        third = screened[2][1]
        t3 = min(250.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.12))
        if t3 > 30:
            a3, o3 = run_fista(
                third, t3, rng,
                tag="sharp",
                beta_init=1000000.0,
                beta_growth=1.0,
                beta_max=5000000.0,
                step_init=0.25,
                step_max=1.0
            )
            if o3 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o3
                best_a = a3.copy()

    # Phase 4: coordinate descent polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and remaining > 90:
        t4 = min(1200.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.42))
        if t4 > 30:
            a4, o4 = run_coordinate_descent(best_a, t4, rng, tag="CD")
            if o4 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o4
                best_a = a4.copy()

    # Phase 5: high-beta FISTA after coordinate descent.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and remaining > 60:
        t5 = min(350.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.20))
        if t5 > 30:
            a5, o5 = run_fista(
                best_a, t5, rng,
                tag="polish",
                beta_init=1000000.0,
                beta_growth=1.0,
                beta_max=5000000.0,
                step_init=0.20,
                step_max=0.8
            )
            if o5 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o5
                best_a = a5.copy()

    # Phase 6: second coordinate pass.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and remaining > 60:
        t6 = min(350.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.18))
        if t6 > 30:
            a6, o6 = run_coordinate_descent(best_a, t6, rng, tag="CD2")
            if o6 < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = o6
                best_a = a6.copy()

    # Phase 7: optional up-sample to larger length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 8192 and remaining > 260:
        n_up = int(min(16384, best_a.size * 2))
        if n_up > best_a.size:
            a_up = normalize_nonnegative(resize_sequence(best_a, n_up), 1.0)
            o_up = exact_objective(a_up)
            print("\n=== Upsample to n=%d, initial obj=%.8f ===" % (n_up, o_up))

            if o_up < best_obj + 0.1:
                t_up = min(700.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.65))
                if t_up > 60:
                    a_up2, o_up2 = run_fista(
                        a_up, t_up, rng,
                        tag="upsF",
                        beta_init=2000.0,
                        beta_growth=1.004,
                        beta_max=2000000.0,
                        step_init=1.0,
                        step_max=3.0
                    )
                    if o_up2 < best_obj - 1e-4:
                        best_obj = o_up2
                        best_a = a_up2.copy()
                        print("Accepted upsampled sequence obj=%.8f" % best_obj)

                        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                        if remaining > 60:
                            t_cd_up = min(300.0, max(0.0, remaining * 0.7))
                            if t_cd_up > 30:
                                a_cd_up, o_cd_up = run_coordinate_descent(
                                    best_a, t_cd_up, rng, tag="upsCD"
                                )
                                if o_cd_up < best_obj - 1e-14:
                                    best_obj = o_cd_up
                                    best_a = a_cd_up.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0

    final_list = finalize_sequence(best_a)

    # One final sanity validation if the evaluator is available.
    eval_fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if eval_fn is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            val = eval_fn(final_list)
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                clear = finalize_sequence(np.ones(best_a.size, dtype=np.float64))
                final_list = clear
        except Exception:
            pass

    print("\nFinal internal objective: %.10f, n=%d" % (best_obj, best_a.size))
    return final_list
```
